2026-11-24T18:08:10.1403013Z But this is not relevant for proofs because of proof irrelevance.
2026-11-24T18:08:10.1403225Z 
2026-11-24T18:08:10.1403396Z Note: This linter can be disabled with `set_option linter.style.haveILetI false`
